# Kawasaki KX: aftermarket parts research catalog

Research candidates gathered from public web sources (manufacturer pages, dealers, retailers, magazine tests, testers, forums). Each product keeps its source and a confidence: high = the manufacturer lists the part number and fitment; medium = a dealer or major retailer does; unverified = inferred or title-only. Nothing here is verified evidence, nothing is shown to riders yet, and no fitment, price or performance claim should be repeated without checking the source. Performance claims are the maker's or tester's, with their conditions.

Files: cg-kawasaki.json, chassis.json, engine.json, kawasaki-research.json, ms-kawasaki.json, suspension.json, tuning.json. Built by scripts/aftermarket/build_catalog.py.

Counts: 5470 products, 22910 part numbers, 28 rider/tester findings, 4 project-bike builds, 26 of 26 in-scope bike-years with at least one mapped product.

## Rider and tester findings

| Bike | Category | Product | Source | Conditions / other mods | What they found | Measured | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Kawasaki KX250 2026 | bike_test | Kawasaki 2026 KX250 review | [Cycle News, Ryan Nitzen, 2025-11-26](https://www.cyclenews.com/2025/11/article/2026-kawasaki-kx250-review/) (magazine) | Aggressive map; PC-modified bike also ridden [] | Unchanged except graphics vs 2025. Forks harsh out of box, engine slightly 'choked' and less revvy than earlier KX250s; 240 lb wet. PC-modified bike: more snap, freer revving. | Fork 2 clicks softer than stock; shock HSC in 1/4 turn. | high |
| Kawasaki KX450X 2026 | engine_package | multiple 2026 KX450X top mods | [Dirt Bike Test, Trevor Hunter, 2026-05-21](https://dirtbiketest.com/fresh-dirt/top-mods-for-a-2026-kx450x/) (magazine) | Fast desert/off-road; ~70 h [] | DT-1 screenless cage + domed filter: more pull, better response. ## Project-bike builds

### [2024 Ride Engineering Kawasaki KX450 Garage Build](https://racerxonline.com/2025/01/24/2024-ride-engineering-kawasaki-kx450-garage-build) — Kawasaki KX450 2024, Racer X 2025-01-24

- chassis: Ride Engineering 23mm offset rubber-mounted split triple clamps Gen 4 — offset/feel; found: Build overall 'opened my eyes up on what I can get out of this new generation Kawasaki chassis' (Keefer); no per-part effect
- chassis: Ride Engineering WP damper with bolt-on steering head bracket — steering damper
- suspension: Ride Engineering Adjustable Performance Link (+0.5/+0.75/+1.0mm inserts) — rear ride height/linkage ratio; found: Tested with +0.5mm
- suspension: ESR Suspension Showa re-valve, 5.2 fork springs (stated as kg/mm), 54 N/mm shock spring — valving/springs
- ecu: XPR Motorsports Mapped Vortex ignition (smooth power map) — mapping
- exhaust: FMF 4.1 slip-on, stock header — exhaust
- intake: DT1 Domed air filter w/ custom carbon cage — airflow
- drivetrain: ProTaper 13/50 sprockets, 520 MX chain, Evo SX Racer bars — stock ratio
- tires: Bridgestone X31 80/100-21 front, X20 120/80-19 rear — tires
- controls: Ride Engineering Anti-twist bar mount, soft yellow cones; braided rear brake line; locking wheel spacers; axle blocks — controls/hardware

### [TUNED: 2025 Kawasaki KX450 Project Bike](https://dirtbiketest.com/fresh-dirt/tuned-2025-kawasaki-kx450-project-bike/) — Kawasaki KX450 2025, Dirt Bike Test 2025-02-07

- suspension: TCS Powersports Suspension package (springs, -3mm shock mod, valving) — springs/valving; found: Better bottoming control
- exhaust: Pro Circuit Ti-6 system (quiet insert kept) — power; found: 'woke up the power tremendously'; quiet insert for back pressure/bottom end
- chassis: GPR V5D Pro stabilizer kit — head shake; found: 'greatly reduces head shake' at speed
- chassis: Works Chassis Lab Engine mounts and hangers — flex tuning; found: Stiff uppers for deep sand, medium for hard pack; stiffer front bracket preferred everywhere
- clutch: Hinson Billetproof clutch — durability; found: Change plates every 10-20 h
- intake: DT-1 Filters Screenless cage + domed filter — airflow; found: Better seal, slight power gain
- drivetrain: Sunstar 13/50 (stock) drivetrain, chain +1 link — gearing/wheelbase; found: 13/52 option for tighter racing
- protection: TM Designworks Chain guide and slider — durability; found: Sturdier than stock
- fuel: IMS 2.8 gal tank with dry-break — range; found: Stock 1.6 gal lasts 45-60 min
- tires: Maxxis / Airmousse MX-IH 80/100-21 front, MX-SI 110/100-18 rear, 140/100-18 Airmousse — off-road racing

### [2025 Kawasaki KX450X Stage 1 Build](https://dirtbiketest.com/fresh-dirt/2025-kawasaki-kx450x-stage-1-build) — Kawasaki KX450X 2025, Dirt Bike Test 2024-11-15

- exhaust: Pro Circuit Ti-6 full system — power; found: 'more aggressive power delivery'
- chassis: Works Chassis Lab Stiff top and bottom motor mounts — chassis feel; found: More comfort and precision
- chassis: Precision Racing Steering damper — head shake; found: Reduces headshake
- intake: DT-1 Filters 3D Air Flow kit (screenless domed) — airflow; found: Slight power, more filter volume
- controls: Pro Taper Contour CR Mid bars — comfort; found: Less front-end harshness
- tires: Maxxis MX-SM scoop 19-in rear, MX-SI 80/100-21 front — sand; found: 19-in more solid in sand whoops
- drivetrain:  13/50 gearing, chain +1 link — West Coast off-road; found: Smoother rear, more stable

### [2024 KX450 Update And Mods That Have Worked](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/?p=27722) — Kawasaki KX450 2024, Keefer Inc Testing 2024-02-04

- ecu: Twisted Development / Vortex Vortex ECU custom map (ETS MX18 fuel) — power; found: 'almost 4 more horsepower' than stock ECU
- exhaust: Pro Circuit Ti-6 Pro full system — power/sound; found: More linear low end; less low end than regular Ti-6 on stock ECU
- intake: Twin Air / custom Filters + open 3D-printed cage, airbox grommet removed, side panel drilled — airflow; found: Better response and low-end feel
- clutch: Hinson Clutch kit (basket, hub, pressure plate, plates) — durability/hookup; found: Longer plate life, slightly wider engagement
- drivetrain: D.I.D ERT3 gold chain — durability; found: Stock chain lasted ~15 h
- suspension: REP Suspension Showa fork/shock valving — hold-up; found: Fork holds up better in braking bumps; shock better HSC/rear hold-up

## Gaps and work not done

- (kawasaki-research.json) FMF maker product pages (fmfracing.com) for 2024+ KX450 / 2025+ KX250 could not be fetched (permission timeout) - no FMF part numbers recorded for this slice.
- (kawasaki-research.json) Kawasaki US accessory page for KX FI Calibration Kit (999990615) fetch failed; Kawasaki AU pages used instead. No Kawasaki-site part numbers for optional fork/shock springs or optional engine mounts were seen.
- (kawasaki-research.json) Ride Engineering product pages failed to fetch (clamp fitment/part numbers not confirmed).
- (kawasaki-research.json) Hinson maker site gave no KX fitment/SKU; Works Connection Hinson page had SKUs but no year mapping.
- (kawasaki-research.json) No 2024+ KX450 piston from Wiseco, Vertex, JE or Pro-X confirmed; Wiseco RE822M09600 stops at 2023.
- (kawasaki-research.json) Pro Circuit Ti-6 Pro item numbers for 2024+ KX450 and 2025 KX250 not seen (page fetch errors).
- (kawasaki-research.json) Akrapovic KX250 page on akrapovic.com not opened; fitment years for S-K2MET10-FDHLTA from dealer titles only.
- (kawasaki-research.json) 2027 KX250F/FX change details not available (Dirt Bike Test first look covers only the 450); 2027 KX450F has new exhaust (longer muffler/header), frame spars, swingarm, Brembo rear - 2024-2026 exhausts and mounts may not carry over. Not confirmed by any parts maker.
- (kawasaki-research.json) Coupler descriptions conflict: Keefer (2020 KX250) says white = better low end; MXA (2021 KX250) says black = strongest low end and white = mid-top; MXA (2022 KX450) says white = same hp, crisper.
- (kawasaki-research.json) Fork-height guidance conflicts: MXA/Cycle News/Dirt Bike Test drop forks flush on 2024+ KX450; Keefer runs 2-3 mm up (and raised 3 mm for stability on his 2024).
- (kawasaki-research.json) MXA 2021 KX250 race test summary described the suspension as Kayaba; this was not checked against Kawasaki specifications.
- (chassis.json) Luxon MX, Neken, Tag Metals, Applied, Emig, Scotts triple clamps: no 2020+ product page reached.
- (chassis.json) Ride Engineering engine mounts, linkages, holeshot devices; Pro Circuit, Moose and Tag holeshot devices: not found.
- (chassis.json) GPR and Tusk steering dampers: no 2020+ MX fitment page reached.
- (chassis.json) OEM optional engine mounts (HRC, GYTR, Kawasaki, KTM): not found, beyond the YZ450FX tester mod.
- (chassis.json) ProTaper, Neken, Tag bars; ODI Podium mounts; ARC, Works Connection and Motion Pro levers/perches; IMS and Pro Circuit pegs: not captured.
- (chassis.json) Galfer rotor kits (KG349 found with no fitment), Brembo, Nissin/Brembo master-cylinder conversions, Galfer/Brembo/SBS pads: no usable fitment.
- (chassis.json) Excel A60, DID Dirt Star, Talon and Haan wheels sold directly: only bundled listings found.
- (chassis.json) TM Designworks, Moose, Polisport protection; Cycra/Acerbis handguards: no 2020+ fitment captured.
- (chassis.json) Selle Dalla Valle and Throttle Syndicate seats/covers: no 2020+ listings found.
- (chassis.json) Triumph TF 450-X/RC, TF 250-C/TF 450-C: only holeshot and skid-plate fitment found.
- (chassis.json) Few 2026-2027 fitments confirmed; most lists end at 2024-2026.
- (engine.json) Hot Cams camshafts: maker store (allballsracinggroup.com) returned in-scope cams only for CRF450R/RX 2020-2022 (single cam), YZ250F/FX 2020-2021 and KTM/Husky 250 4T 2020-2022; no Hot Cams for 2023+ KTM-group, YZ450F 2023+, KX, Triumph found. Note: hotcams.com is now an unrelated adult site - use hotcamsinc.com / allballsracinggroup.com.
- (engine.json) Vertex/Cylinder Works: maker data did not return kits for 2023+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas 4T or 2024+ KX450/YZ450F platforms (may exist; API query cap). Vertex 2026 news lists replica kit 24705 for Triumph TF 250-E/TF 250-X 2025-26 (not added as entry: no fit for 'TF 250-E'; TF 250-X match).
